# Decision-tree coverage checklist (2026-09-30)

Page: `notes/decision_tree/royer_path_v2.html` (SECTIONS data). Backup of the page before this pass:
`/tmp/claude-1000/-home-jovyan-tremorferometry/4419b33c-3b4d-4e80-b616-2e9eca3e7e89/scratchpad/royer_path_v2.backup_1829.html`.

Method: every source file listed below was read in full (split into 14 groups) and each distinct exploration,
test, variant, sweep, fix, bug, dead end, side check and result was listed with its key numbers and source path,
then compared with the tree as it stood before this pass (`in tree` = yes / partial / no **before** the edit).
Sources overlap (the daily notes and the per-study logs describe the same work), so one exploration can appear in
two groups; the per-group numbers are therefore rows, not distinct items.

## What was added to the tree (by section)

Before: 12 trees, 66 questions, 120 option cards, 11 test chips, 2 box cards. After: 27 trees, 165 questions,
414 option cards, 240 test chips, 21 box cards (lint: 0 warnings).

- **Bostock (bosa/bosb)**: bosa Q1–Q5 extended (gap bugs, MAD ranking, pilot 35 % true pairs, merge bugs 9 → 50,
  blind-run void, candidate count 18 → 7 → 1); 2 new questions (pair cc = template cc², all pairs to grouping);
  new sub-trees *Our gated array engine, Step 1* (1a/1b/1c, bed bugs, D0–D7, Merlin audit, 1c′ oracle, mandate change),
  *Learned pickers and template-free discovery* (P0-O, P1b, GATES, P2, P3, P3D, P3D_DISCOVERY, P4-0a, P4-0b; 11 questions),
  *Checking our Washington screens on Bostock's families* (clause-by-clause, literature, tiers, confirmation rule, rank-don't-discard),
  *Locator rebuild* (27.6 → 4.0 km ladder, ablations, double difference, bootstrap). bosb: Track A step 0/1/3 details,
  3 new questions (seasonal false-alarm floor, Track E per-event score, science QC: re-rupture and front estimator),
  sub-trees *Station changes 2014–2026 (Track A-2/C/D)* and *Our own relocation (Track A-2 step 5)*; process box.
- **Royer**: rnw Q1 (papers on disk, blockers, audit chips), new Qs *southern bug in WA code?*, *which velocity model?*,
  *NW 34 % short: why?*, *which muted families are real?*; Q2–Q6 side cards (v1 run, pools, tremor days, R150, all-days,
  headline-cut bug, sweep, star merge, prefetch, pick-ratio sweep, 2–8 Hz picks, hypoDD crash, L9 kill, first union);
  rnwp merge-window card, runaway-guard false alarm, 2338 card, union chips; rsw strict cull, count-match luck, LVZ
  failure, new Q *Hyp2000 ignored trial depth (ZTR)*; rnvi scope omission, NRCan refetch; new sub-trees *A4 seeding at
  Royer's mapped locations*, *What we score against (digitized truth)*, *Earlier: Bostock's recipe on the 2008 Puget
  array*; process-failure box.
- **Chestler**: cc Q1 side cards (Step 5 never run, slowness beam, zero-slowness beam, site-average + old screen) and
  chips (grid edge 46/50, 2011 re-fire, wide-grid move, sealed window, tiers); Q2 rank-don't-discard and Arm B crash;
  Q3 three earlier coordinate tests; Q4 count fixed to 62 (61 with true seconds), duration slip, open check on Arm A
  control; Q5/Q6 chips; new Q7 *Where are the Arm B families?* (43/47, p 0.02); new sub-tree *Preparing the
  Washington array-era data* (census, delivered channels, region/window, probe, tremor hours, withheld stations,
  health mask, sealed 2011).
- **Ducellier**: duca Q1/Q2 chips and also-adopted cards (his code parameters, vertical-only stations, dilution
  numbers); last-gap card marked done; Plourde and Family A plan cards; ducb new Qs *which fixed-detector variant*
  (no-mute 0.959 chosen, muting hurts sparse network), *which stations changed* (63/43 epochs, re-templating
  0.694), *2008 vs his catalogue* (0.504 under his rule).
- **Shelly**: sh Q1–Q7 chips and side cards (queue deaths, channel-count crash, held-out file, ablation, three
  settings, row-D collapse, S1x, v3 deviations, v3b iteration); new Q *which stations carry the families*
  (cc50 GHOP 0.55, KBU 0.53, SPRL 0.51, BRIC 0.49; median 14 of 17 with pick quality ≥10); new sub-trees *Blind
  Mendocino search* (cache-grid bug, B933 screen, 80-day block, MEN model found, result, user stop) and *Forward to
  2026 and back to 2008* (old extension, KMR re-templating, continuity gate 0.718/0.758, same-sensor stations,
  BK1 passed 0.855/0.309, BK2 queued); board updated.
- **Oregon**: chips (outages, BWO0 rates, forced deviations, sanity checks, smoke, shared detections, models
  unavailable, seed-station count, single-event weakness); side cards (3-station floor, ALS0 screen, 99th-percentile
  admission, Hyp2000 smoke); new Qs *code vs paper (12 differences)* and *better Oregon data (ZH, 3F)*.
- **Extension (ext)**: Q1 v1/v1.1 cards and duplicate-test chips; Q2 two carrier bugs and chips; Q3 per-epoch
  thresholds, 2012 trial, OLY:938, site-count precision chips; Q4 T1 Olympic/Arm B, cross-family rule, per-epoch;
  Q5 step-1 in-sample, SW 2011, D1 leak, D3, any-family; Q6 peakedness, group probabilities, chips (215 groups,
  41/114); Q7 T2 iteration, chips (site count, top-3 69 %, bootstrap, coverage); Q8–Q10 chips; new sub-trees
  *Station bridges (plan)*, *Step 4b: adding stations*, *PNET*; tmap rows for PNET fine-tune and confidence D1;
  Z5 box: 7 new cards; new box *The margin plan (09-24): phases and decision points* (reframe, phases 1–4, D1–D4).

## Could not place, or sourced by recomputation

- "Median 14 clear S per family" (Shelly) is not written in any .md; reproduced from
  `lfe_south/results/sh_v3b/tpl_it0.csv` as the per-family count of stations with pick_q ≥ 10 (threshold is our reading).
- WA-trial precision by site count (0.31 / 0.49 / 0.60) is not written in any .md; recomputed from
  `lfe_extension_wa/results/score_val/validation_by_family.csv`.
- `notes/CATALOGUE_SUMMARY.md` §2 calls Bostock 2015's 130-family list unreleased, while the reproduction scored
  against a copy on disk; left out of the tree (contradiction to resolve, not a result).
- `NORTH_DISCOVERY_SUMMARY.md` says the merge cut 50 families to 9; REPRO_BOSTOCK §7 says 50 → 30 (different stages);
  the tree shows "merge → 9 of 96" and "no merge 50 of 96".
- Olympic Arm A count: notes give 62 at 2–8 Hz (61 with true-second durations); the tree now says 62.
- Arm A scrambled control never shifted member stations during growth (notes 09-29): shown as an open check, not verified.
- Sweet et al. 2014, Plourde's front/strike-slip claims, Ducellier's Family A, synthetic injection (A5): never
  attempted, shown as planned cards only.
- Pure logistics with no finding (download tranches, queue pids, cache sizes) were not added.

## Sub-investigations with their own decision sequence (tree lacks entirely)
1. **Matching Royer's first-detection count (NW/SW)**: v1 window vs sample mode (G1-9) → strict overlap cull leaves NW −34 % (G1-12) → shortfall first accepted, then sent back (G1-3) → hour/channel accounting shows complete data → per-window 8×MAD reading fixes it (G1-19) → scrambled-control comparison (G1-38). Tree has only the end state in rnw-q2 / rsw-q1.
2. **Velocity model for Royer**: paper silent → Crosson 1976 provisional with Vp/Vs sweep (G1-15) → sources hunt (G1-24) → GSC 1-D digitized from Savard 2018, NVI smoke confirms band (G1-20) → later USGS v1.7 second column (rsw-q4). Tree has no question for the GSC choice.
3. **Truth and scoring setup**: no catalogue anywhere (G1-24) → digitize figures with georef checks (G1-27) → scorer self-test on Royer's own two catalogues (G1-28) → prior runs scored at chance (G1-31) → figure-filter duplicate bug (G1-76) and depth column bug (G1-48). Tree only mentions digitizing in the section summary.
4. **A4 location-seeded control**: NVI smoke 6/10 (G1-29) → beam resolution failure, ring-contrast fix abandoned (G1-30) → burst attribution → pilot v2 weak (G1-32) → full run not launched. Tree has one [bad] option.
5. **Tremor-day gating and all-days pass**: tremor-day rule (G1-11) → no pre-2009 list, own detector (G1-22) → detector stall fix (G1-33) → user: don't gate on episodes → all-days pass built, cost too high (G1-36) → user: paper steps only, stopped (G1-37). Tree has only the dead detector option.
6. **Growth runaway fixes**: 19 M detections (G1-41) → gap guard two causes (G1-42) → merge rule choice, star vs connected (G1-43) → same guard bug in clustering, SW/NVI rebuilt (G1-44) → merged growth to 240 (G1-45). Tree compresses this to one option in rnw-q4.
7. **Location bottleneck diagnosis (NW)**: first 48 located (G1-46) → pick-ratio sweep (G1-47) → slab-fixed depth (G1-51) → all-station stacking already done (G1-52) → per-detection alignment (G1-53) → record sections and band check (G1-54, G1-55) → 2–8 Hz auto picks worse (G1-58) → eye review (G1-59). Tree rnw-q5 has 4 of these; the record-section, band and pick-ratio steps are missing.

## Sub-investigations with their own decision sequence (missing from the tree)

1. **Velocity-model adoption (USGS CVM v1.7)** — paper models not on disk → user asks for newest model → download and 1-D profile extraction (G2a-21) → Hypoinverse rejects the low-velocity layer, merged upward (G2a-22) → SW three-variant comparison (G2a-23) → Olympic locator comparison (G2a-37) → decision: USGS headline, GSC kept as a column. Tree only has fragments in rsw-q4 and cc-q5.
2. **Z5 Rainier node array** — discovery and access (G2a-46) → geometry vs our families (G2a-47) → timing vs 2025 episodes (G2a-51) → sensor response and day-1 choice (G2a-52) → day-1 test (G2a-54) → batch-2 request (G2a-55). Tree box has only the last two cards.
3. **Bridge/piggy-back station plan** — station inventory and bridge chains (G2a-48) → PH5 retired, no-data flags shown to be sampling artifacts (G2a-50) → ZZ and XD usable, YB mostly not, 3F/Z5 embargoed (G2a-35). Tree has one test-chip in ext-q2.
4. **Better Oregon data** — inventory of 13 later networks and tremor overlap (G2a-34) → 3F nodes embargoed (G2a-35) → conclusion "better data = 3F". Tree has only the END line.
5. **NW fair-merge run operations** — rules (G2a-10) → fast-score check (G2a-11) → runaway guard false alarm and fix (G2a-12) → converged (G2a-14) → no gain (G2a-29). Tree has one card.

## Sub-investigations with their own decision sequence (missing from the tree)

1. **Mendocino blind discovery (sh-q8 is one card only)**: set-up on 25 stations (G2b-4) → cache-grid bug on padded files (G2b-5) → B933 self-repeats hijack autocorrelation, station screen added (G2b-7) → 80-day block because 572 days is unclusterable (G2b-6) → velocity model: USGS box misses the patch, MEN found and trimmed to 15 layers (G2b-8) → result: 6 multi-site families, 0 of 27, likely earthquake repeaters (G2b-10) → user stop, scrambled control never finished (G2b-11).
2. **Catalogue v1.2 carrier audit (ext-q2 shows only the final 496)**: 0-carrier caveat for 31 Olympic families blamed on the filter → found to be a borehole site-key mismatch, fixed 0 → 26 (G2b-14) → separate duration-unit slip found in the site scorers, 53→52 / 62→61 (G2b-15) → Arm B located, 43/47, p = 0.02 (G2b-16) → v1.2 totals, duplicate test not rerun (G2b-17).
3. **Hyp2000 trial-depth flag bug**: flag error noticed → SW re-location test shows no material change (G2b-12) → fixed in ab_hypo.py → knock-on fix in the interface-fixed Olympic script (G2b-13) → MENDO locator inherits the fixed line.
4. **Ducellier fixed re-run variant selection (ducb-q1 has one card)**: adopt his code's hourly MAD + cc×channels rule (G2b-59) → smoke shows burst muting loses his events on the sparse network (G2b-60) → selection rule on his-rule F1 → stage 1 recall 0.905 but pooled precision distorted by his eliminated family, queue restarted to select on F1 (G2b-62) → new-epoch re-templating against 20 scrambled controls (G2b-61).
5. **WA permanent-only trial (ext-q3 has one card)**: validation recall 0.16 with control ≈ data (G2b-19) → island false-alarm thresholds kill recall (G2b-20) → separation grows with site count (G2b-21) → 2012 trial not separable (G2b-23) → step 4b adds 52 stations, 25 → 36 families with 3+ sites, 13–18 separable per episode (G2b-25, G2b-26).

## Sub-investigations with their own decision sequence (3+ dependent decisions, absent from the tree)

1. **WA screen validation and tiered re-screening chain** (RESUME_2026-09-21 §"UPDATE 12:00Z" onward, L34, L108-119): (a) discover the five-clause WA screen keeps only 2 of Bostock's 58 known-real families -> (b) define justified tier A/B/C criteria from first principles -> (c) re-screen WA 2008 (17v2 tier A, 4v0 B/C) and WA 2010 (234v59 tier B, 127v27 tier C) -> (d) find the old interior+10km 2007/2011 confirmation rule is also unjustified (true families relocate a median 35km) -> (e) task agent R to calibrate a confirmation rule on the north half-split and re-confirm 2008->2007 and 2010->2011 under the new tiers (agent R was stopped before launching; redo needed). The tree's bostock section shows only the single "kept 2 of 58" fact (bosa-q4); none of the downstream tier definitions, re-screened counts, or the confirmation-rule calibration chain appear anywhere.

2. **Locator rebuild and revalidation chain** (RESUME_2026-09-21 §11-§12.5, L132, L199-202): (a) old locator gives 27.6km median error / 0.75 rail fraction against Bostock's published coordinates -> (b) rebuild with L9_xcdiff + L12_comb, cutting error to 4.0km and rail to 0.09, raising within-10km families from 11 to 35 of 58 -> (c) test double-difference relocation as a further refinement, measure it worse (7.40 vs 6.44km), reject it -> (d) conclude every WA location column across all beds must be recomputed with the new locator. This is foundational to every location claim in the royer and chestler trees, yet has no locator-methodology node anywhere in the tree.

3. **Puget 2008 "is it the stations or the method" diagnostic chain** (RESUME_2026-09-21 §"UPDATE 10:20Z" through "UPDATE 11:20Z", L28-32, and MARGIN_CATALOG_PLAN D1, L103-106): (a) 2010 Olympic all-station run mostly fails (grid-edge rail) -> (b) hypothesize 64-96 stations in 7 tight clusters cause cluster-noise dominance -> (c) confirm by averaging each site (84/96 stations in 10 clusters), lifting the Olympic ratio 1.39->15.81 -> (d) apply the same site-averaging idea to Puget 2008, expecting a similar lift -> (e) it does NOT lift (ratio stays 0.89) -> (f) run a per-station noise census (CAFE 14dB louder than PB boreholes) and try a quiet-station subset, reaching only 0.86 (boreholes 0.45) -> (g) conclude station quality/noise is not the limiting lever for Puget, and the whole line of inquiry (decision point D1) is left unresolved on its own terms, quietly superseded by the literal Royer-recipe rebuild. Only step (c)'s Olympic-side number (1.39->15.81) reached the tree (chestler cc-q1); the entire Puget-side negative chain (d)-(g) and D1's non-resolution are absent.

## Sub-investigations with their own decision sequence

- **Sweet et al. 2014 downdip-edge single-family control (G3b-31/32/33).** A self-contained sequence
  the tree lacks entirely: (1) build the BS-array third-root/10xMAD recipe, (2) run it 2006-10 to
  2011-09 plus W020/B013/FACB, (3) locate the family and compare to the two plate-interface depth
  offsets Sweet reports, (4) test the four falsifiable behaviours (recurrence, migration,
  moment-vs-waiting-time correlation, post-tremor timing). None of these four dependent steps exists
  anywhere in royer/ext, despite this being flagged as the margin's cheapest positive control.
- **Shelly bug-diagnosis chain (G3b-20/21/22/23), partially in tree.** The source shows four
  dependent findings in sequence — BUG1's exact numeric magnitude (cc to 2.4e15) -> BUG2's exact
  moveout spread (1.49-5.85s) -> a third, separate scoring-denominator fault (recall cap 0.082) ->
  the structural reason Ducellier's chain was immune to both bugs. The tree's sh-q2 compresses this
  into one bullet ("fix three bugs") without the chain of numbers or the immunity explanation, so the
  reasoning sequence itself is not visible even though the top-level fix is credited.

## Sub-investigations with their own decision sequence

1. **The invented network-autocorrelation discovery engine, 2012->2005, Step 1a/1b/1c + diagnostics D0-D7 (G4a-12 to G4a-31, G4a-51 to G4a-58).** A 10+ step dependent chain: build engine -> patch 4 method prerequisites -> smoke-test -> run on the 2012 bed (fails, 0/10) -> discover the dense array is gone by 2012 and build a closer 2012+ bed (still marginal) -> discover the real dense array only existed in 2004-2005 and rebuild the bed there -> hit and fix 3 separate silent bugs (absolute-time indexing, unstable site ids, roster leak) that had been invalidating every station count so far -> run on the 2005 bed (still fails, 0 clusters) -> a full diagnostic pass (D0-D7) traces the failure to one mislabelled earthquake hour plus a real pairing signal that a too-high threshold discards, and an oracle test proves even known-true pairs mostly cannot pass the statistic at any threshold -> engine abandoned, WA discovery parked, project pivots to literally rerunning Bostock's recipe. The tree shows only the single endpoint line (bosa-q1's "gated array engine... led to the literal recipe"); none of the intermediate bed choices, the three silent bugs, or the D3/D5 diagnostic numbers appear anywhere.
2. **The parked WA "Step-5" network-autocorrelation discovery attempt (G4a-43 to G4a-50).** Its own dependent chain: freeze the subregion and cell grid -> bind it onto the frozen engine via an adapter -> discover the admitted-station moveout spread is far too wide and trim the bed by distance -> pick withheld stations under a feasibility-revised rule -> calibrate a station-specific HF veto -> find most timing corrections fail their own precision bar -> design a synthetic-injection test to make Gate 1 testable -> smoke-test the whole chain including injection. WA_GO was never created, so this line of work never appears in the tree at all (the tree's Royer/WA sections describe an unrelated, later literal-recipe rebuild).
3. **The Arm B "jms" clean-control picker thread (G4a-59 to G4a-61).** Model selected as the only non-Cascadia-trained option -> pre-registered acceptance bars -> smoke test -> independent confirmation of a known bad-station tone -> association-threshold cliff measured and a looser rule adopted. Not represented in the tree under any section.

## Sub-investigations with their own decision sequence (not represented at all in the tree at this grain)

1. **The pre-Royer-paper Washington array saga (G4c-1 through G4c-35, agents L/M/N/O/P/Q/S/U/W, 2026-09-21 to 09-23):** a long dependent chain -- discover on 2010 N-Olympic array (thin candidates) -> sealed-2011 confirmation (0/50, cultural repeaters) -> reframe as reproduction failure once the goal became "match the published papers" -> arrays-as-arrays rebuild (Ghosh MBBP beams from Chestler's thesis, still scrambled control) -> station-quality quiet-subset bed (still scrambled control) -> screen-clause validation on the 58 known families (screen was mostly invented, kept only 2/58) -> locator baseline found barely-better-than-chance and rebuilt (27.6 km -> 4.0 km) -> a direction-flag bug reversed the entire "not distinguishable" verdict -> rank-don't-discard rule adopted mid-stream -> the Royer & Bostock full paper finally obtained, revealing every run so far used an unpublished method. The tree currently jumps directly from "0 families matching Royer" (rnw-q1) to "rebuild Royer's method from the paper" with none of this multi-week dead-end arc, its screen-validation numbers, or the locator-rebuild story represented.
2. **Agent AB's pre-launch audit chain (G4c-43 through G4c-49, 2026-09-25):** kill-signal lesson -> gap-bug class audit (ruled out the southern bug, found 3 weaker forms D1-D4) -> gap-indicator column itself found broken -> per-hour vs per-day exposure comparison -> conclusion nothing on record is invalidated -> data-gap re-check (much smaller than assumed) -> full parameter-mismatch table -> code written and cost-estimated, with one explicit open decision (station-pool width) left for review before launch. This whole chain, which directly preceded and justified the successful ab_royer run already in the tree (rnw-q1's "14 code fixes, 3 of them blocking"), has none of its diagnostic sub-steps represented.

## Sub-investigations with their own decision sequence

1. **Step 1 frozen-engine positive control (tree has one card, bosa-q1 "Our gated array engine").** 1a 2012 permanents → 0/10 (distance) → 1b add CN/C8 → 0/10 → 1c 2005 POLARIS → 0/10 (T 12.5) → diagnostics D0–D7 (one-earthquake threshold, NaN void, pair list enriched 6× but single pairs never significant) → Merlin audit (SHVB tone, quiet-hour clock bias, seed rule) → 1c′ repair → oracle C_30 = 0 → Merlin ruling (whole-second times broke the oracle; final staged-survival spec) → abandoned for the literal published recipe. Rows G5-32 … G5-63. Suggest a new tree, or one bosa question "Why did our gated engine find nothing?".
2. **Screen and confirmation validation on known families.** Clause-by-clause on 58 families → reformulations (cap 0.25, Δz 19 km, matched base rate) → tiers A/B/C → WA 2008 / 2010 re-screens → half-split confirmation calibration (≥1 day, FA ≤1/day; location clause dropped). Rows G5-76 … G5-92. The tree has one card (bosa-q4 "Apply our Washington screen").
3. **Array-era site preparation for WA (N-Olympic 2010 / Puget 2008).** Merlin site count → region/window match → metadata 3-C audit (ZK Z-only, XG SL dead) → tremor contrast probe (97/131; CL fails) → coverage 94.6 % → withheld rule infeasible → Option B. Rows G5-64 … G5-75. Not in the tree at all.

## Sub-investigations with their own decision sequence

1. **Bostock recipe on the Washington 2008 Puget bed** (G6-1 to G6-7): replace the diurnal hour flag with a waveform audit → 51-station run (ratio 0.89) → five-clause screen (0 vs 0) → per-station noise census → quiet-subset and boreholes-only reruns (0.86 / 0.45) → conclusion that noisy summed stations sink the recipe. The tree has only one line (rnw-q1 "Reuse our earlier Washington pipeline"). Suggested new question in rnw.
2. **Array-site treatment on the 2010 Olympic arrays** (G6-15 to G6-21): sites by geometry → slowness beam not identifiable → coherence measured on earthquakes → member/beam/site-average choice → full site-average run and screen → 2011 re-fire. The tree keeps only the final 1.39 → 15.81 line (cc-q1).
3. **Locator rebuild calibrated on Bostock's published coordinates** (G6-36 to G6-42): baseline 27.6 km → pick residual diagnosis → ladder L0–L13 → L9 adopted (4.0 km) → ablations → double difference not adopted → bootstrap radius → Washington relocation → 280-rich test. Missing from the tree; suggested new question in bosa ("How accurate are our family locations?").
4. **Washington bug-class audit** (G6-23 to G6-27): southern bug absent → D1–D4 defects → cache gap column wrong → verdict that no number is retracted → mask carried into the Royer chain. Missing; suggested new question in rnw.
5. **Final-assessment ranking redesign** (G6-28, G6-29, G6-32 to G6-35): rank-don't-discard rule → AUC per criterion → confirmation rule calibrated on Bostock (weak) → depth window rejected → only the ≥5-station clause passes. The tree has only the flipped-column result (cc-q2).
6. **A4 location-seeded positive control** (G6-86 to G6-90): smoke → pilot v1 stopped by the shared burst → ring contrast abandoned → burst attribution → pilot v2. The tree has only the pilot result (rnw-q2).

| G7-25 | P2 ADDENDUM (post-review correction): the moveout-shuffle count-based scrambled control is REVERSED — the wrong travel-time table actually recovers 60-70% of the true detector's chance-corrected recall, so P2 is formally closed as a locator/discovery engine; the "61% far from known families" figure equals the uniform-grid expectation and is withdrawn; a loud-ETS-day normalisation bug is found that understates the reported recall | shuffled/true ratio 0.60-0.71 (bar was <=0.10); uniform-grid expectation 64.5% vs observed 59-61%; loudest days: 5-10 detections/day vs 1,169-2,262 events (26-50x collapse) while template stays flat | lfe_picker/results/P2_RESULTS.md ADDENDUM | 2026-09-19 | no | new question in bosa: "is P2 really a locator/discovery engine once correctly nulled?" | status=bad; h=P2 CLOSED as locator/discovery engine; r=Two of P2's headline discovery claims were re-derived from its own stored outputs after review: the location-geometry scrambled control actually fails once measured the right way, and the '61% away from known families' number turns out to be exactly what randomly-placed detections would give anyway; n=shuffled keeps 60-70% of recall; log=lfe_picker/results/P2_RESULTS.md ADDENDUM |

| G7-28 | P3 Step1a robustness checks before accepting the STOP: an in-distribution scrambled control still gives 0/10; giving the search the true cell/window (oracle placement) buys only ~3x; the pair statistic is 14x weaker than a template match by the expected root-N penalty (the physical reason for the STOP); the alias (neighbour-family) channel would dominate if the threshold were loosened | in-distribution scrambled control 86 pairs/0.0e-5 (still 0/10, max 3); oracle placement enrichment 2.8x-6.0x vs 1.9x baseline; pair-vs-stack excess +0.32 vs +4.6 dB (14x weaker); alias rate 65%/53% of same-family rate at loose thresholds | lfe_picker/results/P3_RESULTS.md §2.3 | 2026-09-19 | no | new question in bosa | status=dead; h=Four checks confirm the pairwise-linkage STOP; r=Before accepting that direct event-to-event linkage cannot work, four separate audits were run to rule out a broken scrambled control, bad window placement, a broken statistic, or a rescuable alias channel; all four confirmed the same negative for the same underlying reason (correlating one noisy event against another, not against a stack); n=pair statistic 14x weaker than template match; log=lfe_picker/results/P3_RESULTS.md §2.3 |

| G7-78 | Track A-2 Step4: a negative control (a station with NO instrument change at all, tested across the same year gap) reveals the pre-registered absolute epoch-identity bars (correlation >=0.6, registration >=0.8x) are unusable as written, because that no-change station fails them too — they measure change in the event population between a 2012 slow-slip episode and a later one, not instrument identity | negative control (B926, no boundary): cc ratio 0.79, registration ratio 0.66, only 5% of its own control-OK families pass the absolute bars | lfe_extension/results/TRACK_A2_RESULTS.md Step4 | 2026-09-16 | no | new question in bosb: "are the pre-registered instrument-change identity bars actually usable?" | status=bad; h=No-change control shows the identity bars are unusable as written; r=Ran the exact same before/after instrument-change test on a station that never changed instruments at all, as a control; it failed the pre-registered pass bars almost as badly as some real instrument changes, proving the bars measure something else (event-population change over time) and every real result had to be re-judged relative to this control; n=control cc ratio 0.79, registration 0.66; log=lfe_extension/results/TRACK_A2_RESULTS.md |

| G7-93 | Track D: a multi-step diagnosis rules out simple noise-adaptivity as the cause of the seasonal false-alarm instability, shows the floor is DECOUPLED from how loud the ambient ground is (winter is louder every year yet not always the worse season), and a decisive withheld-station test with a purpose-built moveout-shuffle control confirms genuine, if modest, coherent signal in the "quiet-day" detections in both seasons | family-day noise-scale (MAD) moves <2% between seasons despite ambient noise energy being 8-61% louder in winter every epoch; withheld-station excess above the moveout-shuffled scrambled control: DJF +0.17 to +0.59dB (p~0.048), JJA +0.51 to +0.74dB (p~0.048) | lfe_extension/results/trackD/RESULTS.md §1d | 2026-09-17 | no | new question in bosb: "what actually drives the seasonal false-alarm instability, and are the extra quiet-day detections real signal?" | status=part; h=Seasonal floor instability diagnosed as a coherent-transient population, not noise; r=Ran a multi-part diagnosis (tail-shape check, noise-decoupling check, within-season slope check, and a decisive withheld-station test with a new moveout-specific control) to find out why the false-alarm floor varies unpredictably by season; it is not simply louder ground raising the detector's own noise floor, and the withheld-station test shows there is genuine, if modest, real coherent signal at the family-specific arrival time in both seasons; n=noise-scale flat (<2% change) despite loud/quiet season; log=lfe_extension/results/trackD/RESULTS.md §1d |

## Sub-investigations with their own decision sequence

- **lfe_picker's whole template-free-discovery chain (P0-O -> P1b -> P2 -> P3 -> P3D -> P3D_DISCOVERY -> P4-0/0a -> P4-0b), G7-1 through G7-63.** An 8-stage, strictly gated pre-registered sequence (each stage's licence to proceed depends on the previous stage's numeric verdict) testing whether LFE families can be discovered without a pre-existing catalogue, first proven against Bostock's known north network and then sized for a Puget re-scope. The tree's bosa-q1 currently represents this entire chain as two flat bullet points (learned pickers "recall 0.22-0.34x", permanent-network discovery "0 of 18 families assembled"), missing every intermediate decision, correction, retraction, and the final cross-cutting Merlin synthesis verdict. This deserves its own tree branch or a dedicated sub-tree mirroring bosa/bosb's structure.
- **Track A-2's epoch-identity-and-relocation investigation (G7-78 through G7-86).** A dependent sequence: build a no-change negative control -> discover the pre-registered absolute bars are miscalibrated -> re-derive relative bars against the control -> independently re-derive station timing corrections from earthquakes (exposing an earlier circularity) -> test the location-accuracy bar on synthetic data -> run the real relocation -> arrive at a per-family accepted-location list. None of this appears anywhere in the tree, even though the "bosa/bosb" board line's `model` field ("Bostock's published hypocentres") implies our own relocation was never adopted — a fact the tree does not explain or reference.
- **Track D's seasonal false-alarm-floor instability investigation (G7-92, G7-93, plus the NOTES.md-only early-check reversal G7-99).** A dependent diagnostic sequence: the pre-registered winter/summer stability bar fails -> rule out tail-shape and noise-adaptivity as causes -> show the floor is decoupled from ambient noise level -> show within-season the loudest days do NOT fire more -> run a decisive withheld-station test with a purpose-built moveout-shuffle scrambled control -> conclude the floor tracks a season-varying population of coherent transients with no fixed sign. Entirely absent from the tree's bosb branch.
- **Track E's per-event realness-score investigation (G7-105 through G7-113).** A dependent sequence: build and self-check a per-site re-computation -> select a model architecture -> fit and validate calibration (which fails) -> diagnose the failure as a level shift from a genuine network-strength effect -> ablate feature groups -> apply the score to the full catalogue -> run an independent out-of-sample check -> discover an unresolved contradiction with Track D's seasonal finding -> discover the cc-normalisation bug along the way. Entirely absent from the tree; Track E and its `p_real`/`hp` machinery is referenced only implicitly (e.g. via "confidence" concepts in the `ext` tree) without ever being named as its own investigation.

## Sub-investigations with their own decision sequence (missing from the tree)

1. **Southern station epochs → re-templating (Ducellier extension, ducb):** map every FDSN epoch since 2008 (G8-57) → decide which epochs keep his template (sensor swaps he ran through) → test re-templating on the known answer by leaving 6 stations out (G8-58) → gate each rebuilt template with the scrambled control → re-scan affected months (pass 2). The tree's ducb has only one question.
2. **Ducellier fixed-detector variant choice (ducb):** re-read his code (hourly MAD, cc×nchannel rule; G8-54) → smoke shows burst muting hurts on the sparse network (G8-56) → known answer scores four variants (G8-55) → carry "nomute" by the rule "≥ 0.836 and higher pooled F1".
3. **Shelly backward extension (sh):** same-sensor station screen (6 of 17; G8-46) → BK1 known-answer test with S6 and S2 station sets at 8/9/10× (G8-47) → gate precision ≥ 0.5 PASS → BK2 2008–2017 queued after the forward scan (G8-48). The tree shows these only as "next" items.
4. **Shelly forward extension (sh):** station-epoch decisions (13 epochs, KMR re-templated twice; G8-50) → continuity gate on held-out 2024 days (G8-49, PASS) → scan 2023-12 → 2026-09. It replaces the superseded unfixed extension (G8-51).
5. **Southern pipeline plumbing failures (sh/duca):** overnight queue death (G8-15) → true cause was a channel-count change, not vertical-only stations (G8-16) → missing held-out day file found before it fired (G8-17) → heartbeat and failure markers added to every queue.
6. **Oregon detector port (or):** find Savard's code (G8-61) → log code-vs-paper differences and follow the code (G8-69) → forced station floor of 3 (G8-65) → ALS0 spectral-screen loss (G8-64) → Q1 full-set quirk resolved by the user in favour of the paper (or-q3).

Sources read in full: lfe_extension_wa/{README.md, PLAN.md, NOTES.md}, results/{WA_TRIAL, STEP4B, MERLIN_TESTS, STEP1_PROB, STEP2_GROUPS, PNET, CONFIDENCE, T2B}_RESULTS.md, results/stack_activity/STACK_ACTIVITY_RESULTS.md.

## Sub-investigations with their own decision sequence (missing from the tree)

1. **Step 4b, add stations** (G9-11 → G9-14 → G9-15 → G9-16/17 → G9-23): precision rises with site count → add 52 stations (projection 28) → measured 25 → 36 families with 3+ sites → 3–5 more separable families per episode, pass 2 adds nothing → the same stations lower stacking skill. The tree shows it only as a test-chip on ext-q3.
2. **Confidence design D1 → D2 → D3** (G9-39 → G9-55 → G9-56/58 → G9-60): step 1 perfect in-sample only → spec fit on the build half leaks → refit on the test half by day-folds (AUC 0.87, but 2011 totals run 25–30 % high) → control-as-negatives gives only a small gain. The tree has only the chosen D2 option in ext-q5.
3. **PNET route** (G9-51 → G9-52 → G9-54 → G9-63): off-the-shelf fails (~0 on 1-C) → fine-tune passes the pre-set rule → no gain inside step 1 or as candidate times → the top input of the confidence model, but gains only at trained stations. The tree scatters this across ext-q5 and ext-q7 without the fine-tune or held-out-station result.
4. **Per-epoch threshold attempts** (G9-10 → G9-17 → G9-32): the Vancouver Island false-alarm rule fails in the trial, in step 4b and in T1 (~18–22×MAD, recall ≈ 0), which is why separation counts replaced thresholds. The tree has none of these.
